# Contradictions register

Status: Open work: documents to correct

When a listed document is corrected (or deleted), remove its entries; delete this file when none are left.
Owner: Repository maintainers
Code: `21d439536dde221fe072757ac939c3eb6fccbe6d` · Rubric `530601c0db44` · Generated 2026-09-27

Every place where an earlier audit, tracker, ledger, decision record or product
page disagrees with the code at `21d4395`. Counts and every table in §1, §3
and §4 are computed by `a build script from the audit run (not kept in the repository)` from
`audit/ledger/ecosystem-capability-ledger.jsonl` (each item's `priorClaims` verdicts), `claims/*.jsonl` and
`the audit working data (not kept in the repository)`; §2 is written by hand, with code evidence re-read at
the SHA.

**Verdicts.** _Confirmed_: the claim agrees with the audited cell. _Contradicted_:
it disagrees. _Not comparable_: the claim is about something the mapped item does
not measure (a different surface, property or granularity). A claim mapped to
several items is counted once, as contradicted if any mapping contradicts it
(120 claims have mixed verdicts across items). Across
7,655 claim-to-item comparisons:
2,771 confirmed, 1,233 contradicted, 3,651 not comparable.

**Doc action.** A _living_ document (Status: Current, or a register or ledger
that is edited as work lands) is corrected in place. A _dated snapshot_ is left
as written and gets a dated reconciliation note that points to the CR entries
here. Documents outside the repository (local-only checklists, security scan
directories, the Codex copy of the filled checklist) get the note in their
successor report under `audit/reports/`.

**Production caveat.** Findings that depend on a migration above 0273 assume the
tracked ledger (`scripts/config/production-migrations-applied.json`) is right.
C-001 (CR-008) records the conflicting 0291 report.

## 2. High-impact contradictions

Ordered by the harm a wrong statement can do: first, copy a customer reads today; then production state and security verdicts; then decision and status records that steer engineering; then systemic over-claims in the audit sources themselves. Every code citation is at `21d4395`. "Doc to update" says whether the target is a **living** document (edit it in place) or a **dated snapshot** (leave the text alone and add a dated reconciliation note that points here).

### A. Customer-facing copy contradicted by behaviour

#### CR-001 · /desktop says "Every device step asks"

- **Source:** `apps/web/app/desktop/page.tsx:148-154`, marketing page (product code, live).
- **Claimed:** "Every device step asks. Folders, programs, the screen and the pointer are granted one at a time … A step you did not approve does not run."
- **Code shows:** `requestPermission` returns any existing grant without prompting (`apps/desktop/electron/runtime/permissionManager.ts:299-300`). The prompt offers "Allow this session", and for high-risk capabilities the grant "lasts until you quit the app" (`permissionManager.ts:302-319`). One computer-use grant then covers every click and keystroke; nothing pauses before a payment, password or send (`apps/desktop/electron/runtime/dispatcher.ts:413-434`, `:829-867`). Emergency stop (`computer_stop`) has no caller, so the only stop is the app-menu "Take Over" item (`apps/desktop/electron/appMenu.ts:115-116`).
- **Verdict:** Contradicted. Ledger S65.23 and S70.21 desktop are both partial, and S2.09 web is partial with this copy named.
- **Doc to update:** the marketing page, a living product surface. Either reword it to "each capability asks once per session", or add a per-step confirmation for high-risk actions.

#### CR-002 · Site nav says Desktop does "Local models, keys and connectors on your machine"

- **Source:** `apps/web/features/marketing/components/system/nav.ts:31-35`.
- **Claimed:** AGI Desktop is "Local models, keys and connectors on your machine".
- **Code shows:** the /desktop page contradicts its own nav entry: "The current Desktop contract is cloud-only for inference. Local model servers and provider keys are not accepted" (`apps/web/app/desktop/page.tsx:136-137`). `docs/decisions/wire-or-cut.md:15-17` records Electron as Managed Cloud only with `localMcp: false`, so local connectors (stdio MCP) don't run on desktop either (S58.02 and S58.05 desktop are declined).
- **Verdict:** Contradicted on all three nouns. `nav.ts` is outside the marketing-claims guard, which is how the line survived.
- **Doc to update:** `nav.ts` (living). Also add `nav.ts` to the claims guard's scope.

#### CR-003 · Pricing says Chrome needs Pro; the server lets Free use it

- **Source:** `apps/web/app/pricing/page.tsx:216-218` ("Developer surfaces" column: "CLI, Chrome & VS Code" for Pro and above, "No managed access" below) and `apps/web/features/billing/lib/plan-display.ts:54-55` ("Managed CLI, Chrome, and VS Code access" as a Pro feature).
- **Code shows:** `getCloudChatSurfaceCapability('chrome')` returns `managed_chat` (`apps/web/lib/free-chat-surface-policy.ts:88-96`), and `managed_chat` is granted to every tier from Free up (`billing-catalog.ts:208,214`). Only `vscode` and `cli` map to `developer_surfaces`.
- **Verdict:** Contradicted. The copy and the gate disagree, so S81.09 web and desktop are partial.
- **Doc to update:** pricing and plan-display copy (living). This needs a **product decision** first: either move Chrome out of the "Developer surfaces" wording, or gate Chrome as `developer_surfaces`.

#### CR-004 · /cli says "The CLI is released"

- **Source:** `apps/web/app/cli/page.tsx:201`. The same phrase, "released CLI", appears in `about/page.tsx:53`, `agi-code/page.tsx:157`, `business/page.tsx:86,126`, `desktop/page.tsx:141`, `enterprise/page.tsx:188`, `docs/byok-env/page.tsx:126`, `data-use/page.tsx:62`, `connectors/mcp-directory/page.tsx:101` and the connectors API error at `apps/web/app/api/connectors/route.ts:495`.
- **Code shows:** `CLI_IS_PUBLISHED: boolean = false` (`apps/extension-vscode/src/integrations/localRuntimeClient.ts:74`). `.github/cross-version-matrix.json:9` records that the npm package `@agiworkforce/cli` "is scaffolded but unpublished; the registry 404s". `/download` lists archives only when a GitHub `v-cli-*` release exists (`apps/web/app/download/CliDownloadAvailability.tsx:82`).
- **Verdict:** Contradicted for npm. Whether a GitHub binary release exists is a production-state check, so S2.11 web stays unverified and is in the live queue. The consequence is wide: Electron local coding sessions spawn `agi` from PATH, and VS Code sidebar chat drives the same binary, so both depend on a CLI users can't install (the `needs-local-cli` tag in the status doc).
- **Doc to update:** the eleven copy sites above (living). Either publish, or change the copy to "build from source" until `CLI_IS_PUBLISHED` flips.

#### CR-005 · Twilio connector advertises "voice calls"

- **Source:** `apps/web/features/connectors/data/connectors.ts:717-720` (`capabilitySummary: 'SMS, WhatsApp messages, and voice calls'`).
- **Code shows:** there is no telephony anywhere. §51 has 0 done and 0 partial cells. S51.16 ("place authorized outbound phone calls") and S51.12 ("provision a phone number") are missing on web, desktop, mobile and the API. The only Twilio code is a chat connector with no pinned endpoint, and its directory record describes Twilio's documentation MCP (`apps/web/lib/connectors/directory/sources/vendor-directory.json:7407`).
- **Verdict:** Contradicted.
- **Doc to update:** `connectors.ts` (living). Describe what the connector does in chat, not telephony.

#### CR-006 · Temporary chat promises nothing is written to history

- **Source:** in-product copy at `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ComposerPlusMenu.tsx:366-368` and `apps/web/features/settings/sections/PrivacySection.tsx:736-744`.
- **Claimed:** no message is written to history, and ending the chat removes every message on any device.
- **Code shows:** the first turn of a new temporary chat is persisted. `useChatStream` computes the temporary flag from the placeholder id and never recomputes it (`apps/web/lib/hooks/useChatStream.ts:3247-3251`), and the free-pool route saves `user_message` without a temporary check. `/api/search` then returns temporary titles and messages because it has no `is_temporary` filter (`apps/web/app/api/search/route.ts:256-262`, `:305-313`). The Codex live run reproduced it (finding LQA-01). Mobile search uses the same route.
- **Verdict:** Contradicted. This is a **privacy defect**: S41.03 web/desktop and S34.02 web/desktop/mobile are partial.
- **Doc to update:** fix the code rather than the copy; the copy states the intended contract. Also add the defect to `audit/registers/known-flaws.md` (living).

#### CR-007 · Three more in-product promises the behaviour breaks

| Surface copy                                                                            | Claimed                                                             | Code shows                                                                                                                                                    | Ledger                     |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------- |
| `apps/web/features/connectors/components/ConnectorConsentSummary.tsx:7-10`              | "every tool call in that turn waits for your approval … every time" | Scheduled runs use approval mode `auto`, and a saved Allow skips the prompt (`apps/web/app/api/connectors/permissions/route.ts:59-69`)                        | S55.27 web/desktop partial |
| Schedule form (`apps/web/features/schedules/components/SchedulesPage.tsx:669`)          | runs don't use memory or tools                                      | the executor loads account memory and offers web search, code tools and Always-allow connectors (`apps/web/lib/services/scheduled-agent-executor.ts:588-593`) | S63.06 web/desktop partial |
| Mobile Delete all (`apps/mobile/src/features/settings/data-controls/index.tsx:167-195`) | chats are "permanently deleted. This cannot be undone"              | the server soft-deletes, and the chats can be restored on web under Recently deleted (`apps/web/app/api/chat/conversations/bulk/route.ts:53-66`)              | S85.18 mobile partial      |

- **Verdict:** Contradicted, all three.
- **Doc to update:** the three copy sites (living). Fix the code or the copy; pick per product decision.

### B. Production state

#### CR-008 · C-001: WEB_PUBLIC_RELEASE_AUDIT says production is at migration 0291; the tracked ledger says 0273

**Resolved 2026-09-27.** A live `pnpm db:migrate -- status` against production showed 291 applied, 3 pending and 0 drift, so the web release audit was right and `scripts/config/production-migrations-applied.json` (273) was wrong. 0292 to 0294 were then applied; production is at 0294 and the record is updated.

- **Source:** `WEB_PUBLIC_RELEASE_AUDIT.md:46-49` (claim `web-release-audit:L48`; Status Current; last updated 2026-09-22): "superseded by the September 22 production version response reporting `0291`".
- **Code shows:** `scripts/config/production-migrations-applied.json:3-4` has `"appliedThrough": 273, "recordedAt": "2026-09-20"`. The audit follows the config, since tracked config outranks prose.
- **Verdict:** Unresolved conflict between the two documents. It can't be settled from a checkout. It is the first production-state check in the live-verification queue.
- **Why it matters:** these findings all rest on migrations between 0274 and 0293 being pending:
  - every cloud scheduled or triggered run fails (0284);
  - every server memory write except mobile sync fails (0285);
  - enterprise agreement and invoice reads fail (0286-0288);
  - the identity risk engine is blind (0280);
  - overage accounting is corrupt (0281);
  - recovery-pending is absent (0277);
  - plugin release history is missing (0289);
  - CMK re-wrap fails after rotation (0290);
  - route pinning is unavailable (0293).

  If 0291 is real, these flip, and the status doc carries a sensitivity table for exactly this case.

- **Doc to update:** whichever is wrong. If production really reports 0291, update the living ledger `scripts/config/production-migrations-applied.json`, which its own guard owns. Otherwise correct L46-49 of the living `WEB_PUBLIC_RELEASE_AUDIT.md`.

### C. Security-scan and re-audit verdicts

Sources:

- **security-scan-0909:** `CLAUDE-SECURITY-20260909-050816/`, scoped to `apps/web/app/api`, baseline `e2a9e898`.
- **security-scan-0816:** `docs/remediation/security-scan-2026-08-16/`, untracked; a nested `.gitignore` holds `*`.
- **CORRECTIONS:** `docs/work/checklist-reaudit/CORRECTIONS.md`, local-only, baseline `c8ed5c61`.

All three are **dated snapshots that are not in the repository**, so each gets a reconciliation note in its successor report under `audit/reports/`, not an edit. The re-audit called the migration findings release blockers that were "not live" while production was at 0248. Production is now recorded at 0273, so each one is either fixed or live.

The "not live" framing is stale                                              |

**Related, found by lanes, not in either scan:**

- live voice attaches hosted `web_search` and `code_interpreter` with `requiresApproval: false` (`live-voice-tools.ts`);
- `POST /api/plugins/authored` bypasses the authoring flag and org plugin policy;
- `llm/v1` fails open when the model-policy read fails (`request-processor.ts:1628-1650`, founder decision pending).

These belong in the next scan's scope note.

### D. Stale decision and status records

#### CR-022 · wire-or-cut "Embedded MCP Apps host: Cut" is stale

- **Source:** `docs/decisions/wire-or-cut.md:206-210` (dated 2026-07-30). It cuts the MCP Apps host because two unreachable Desktop prototypes "never implemented the MCP Apps host contract".
- **Code shows:** web has a complete MCP Apps host:
  - `InteractiveCardBlock.tsx:38` mounts `McpAppCard`, which implements initialization and the host bridge (`apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/cards/McpAppCard.tsx:139-153,261,301`);
  - the tool loop emits the app resource (`tool-loop.ts:2159`);
  - S58.17 to S58.23 are done on web and desktop (hosted).
- **Verdict:** Contradicted. The cut was right for the Tauri prototypes, but the line now reads as "no MCP Apps host".
- **Doc to update:** `wire-or-cut.md` is a living decision ledger. Don't rewrite the dated row; add a dated superseding row ("Wire: web MCP Apps host, McpAppCard") under a new heading.

#### CR-023 · wire-or-cut "Per-project memory scope: Cut" is stale

- **Source:** `docs/decisions/wire-or-cut.md:423`. It says the project-memory select had one option and scoping "exists nowhere".
- **Code shows:**
  - migration 0135 adds project-scoped memory, and the server honours it (`apps/web/lib/services/managed-memory-context-service.ts:825-829`);
  - `ProjectSettingsDialog.tsx:256-270` renders a real `usesGlobalMemory` control;
  - but the list/read contract drops `usesGlobalMemory` (`packages/contracts/cloud-contracts/src/projects.ts:13-38`), so the checkbox always opens ticked and re-saves `true`.
- **Verdict:** Contradicted. The feature exists and is partial (S23.11 web/desktop), not cut.
- **Doc to update:** `wire-or-cut.md` (add a superseding row). Also carry the contract bug in `PRODUCT_GAPS.md` (living).

#### CR-024 · The training-choice decline rests on a false premise

- **Source:** `docs/decisions/wire-or-cut.md:486`. It upholds `GAP-032` because "customer content is never used for training (`apps/web/app/privacy/page.tsx:133,447`)". `audit/ui-gaps.csv:263` (`GAP-259`) records the "Improve the model" toggle removed as a dead control.
- **Code shows:**
  - the Free plan's own disclosure answers "Not by AGI. Free model providers may." (`apps/web/lib/compliance/free-plan-training-disclosure.ts:3,8`);
  - the privacy page narrows its claim to "AGI-owned models" (`apps/web/app/privacy/page.tsx:873`; the cited lines 133 and 447 no longer hold that text);
  - Free-plan traffic goes to providers' free models, which may train. An opt-out toggle exists (`keepOutOfProviderTraining` in `apps/web/features/settings/sections/PrivacySection.tsx`, enforced for chat in `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/request-processor.ts`); on the Free plan it refuses chat because no Free model qualifies, and enforcement is incomplete on other paths, which lane 19 records (`WEB-PROVIDER-TRAINING-SETTING-01` in `audit/registers/known-flaws.md`).
- **Verdict:** Contradicted premise. S85.12 web/desktop/mobile is **missing**, with a product decision needed.
- **Doc to update:** `wire-or-cut.md` (superseding row) and `audit/ui-gaps.csv` GAP-259 (living). The decision itself is the founder's call.

#### CR-025 · execution-state L111 and L112 (desktop computer use and dictation)

- **Source:** `docs/development/execution-state.md` (Status Current; last updated 2026-09-06):
  - **L111:** "Desktop computer use, local voice, OS permissions: no transport from the shipped Electron build to the Rust layer (D-15 spike queued)."
  - **L112:** "Desktop dictation into another app does not exist; system wide dictation fail closed by design record."
- **Code shows:**
  - For L111, Electron has its own computer-use path, with no Rust transport needed: screenshot, zoom, move, click, type, scroll and drag (`apps/desktop/electron/runtime/dispatcher.ts:829-867`), plus the session grant (`permissionManager.ts:292-320`).
  - For L112, it's true that nothing dictates into other apps (S49.28 confirmed), but no decision row records "fail closed by design", so the cell stays missing rather than declined.
- **Verdict:** L111 is contradicted. L112's fact is confirmed, but its cited rationale is unsupported.
- **Doc to update:** `execution-state.md` (living; edit L111 in place, and make L112 point at a real decision row or drop "by design").

### G. Systemic over-claims in the audit sources

#### CR-043 · The filled checklist from the prior Claude chat over-claims systematically

- **Source:** the filled inventory checklist from an earlier Claude chat. Its only copy is the Codex `source-checklist.txt`. It has 3,435 claims and no evidence paths or baseline.
- **Measured (script, from the ledger):**
  - 969 of the 3,126 comparable claims are contradicted (31.0%), out of 3,161 mapped. That is the highest contradiction volume of any source.
  - 575 of the contradicted claims were `[x]` implemented.
  - Direction, against the item's best audited cell: 464 over-claim, 335 under-claim, 170 mixed.
- **Share of "implemented" claims per surface where the audited cell is only partial or missing:**

  | Surface  | Share | Partial + missing / audited |
  | -------- | ----- | --------------------------- |
  | Chrome   | 43%   | 18 / 42                     |
  | Mobile   | 41%   | 31 / 76                     |
  | Desktop  | 38%   | 73 / 192                    |
  | Web      | 30%   | 302 / 1015                  |
  | Platform | 26%   | 80 / 307                    |
  | CLI      | 25%   | 29 / 117                    |
  | VS Code  | 12%   | 4 / 32                      |

- **Desktop credit from Tauri:** 22 contradictions explicitly credit the public desktop from Tauri-only code, which is internal and not shipped:
  - Monaco and xterm (S104.01, S104.T09);
  - the AGI Code IDE panels (S66.08, .09, .11, .12, .29, .31, .32);
  - push-to-talk and the microphone picker (S48.12, S48.18);
  - local-only store and clear (S41.14, .16, .17);
  - MCP edit and local servers (S58.04, S58.05);
  - window targeting (S65.05);
  - PTY (S99.17);
  - approved-folder management (S86.16);
  - the theme editor (S32.11);
  - the send-key preference (S84.14);
  - the usage summary (S18.23).

  The pattern: an unqualified "Desktop" marker was granted on the strength of `apps/desktop/src` and `src-tauri`.

- **Heaviest sections:** §101 (35), §6 (25), §66 (25), §23 (19), §57 (19), §68 (19).
- **Verdict:** Unreliable as evidence. Use it only as a list of places to look.
- **Doc to update:** none in the repository; the document lives outside it. Record this reliability note wherever it is cited, and never let it justify a `done` cell.

Tool-computed from the claims alone (`the audit working data (not kept in the repository)`), before any code
was read: 730 conflicts. Status conflicts are listed by document pair
only; the ledger's per-item verdicts above resolve each one against code.

| scope           |    21 | an all-surface claim whose evidence names one surface only            |
| doc-vs-decision |    11 | a document reports a gap that a decision record has declined          |
